# Original explicit corrected-lookahead and drafting control

The first preparation widened historical protocol admission and failed its existing test. The corrected V2-only parser then refused the first pilot before model allocation because its uncorrected request conflicted with the installed correction pin. Preserve both refusals; neither is evidence of a memory-plan limit.

The prospective continuation introduces explicit enabled original lookahead only for V2 pilots, authenticates the shipped correction, reserves its complete rounded cost and preserves original Auto and old protocols. Its plan-only command admits two streamed drafts and corrected lookahead within the same ten-GB ceiling. The unchanged coding rubric, physical watchdog, real admission, quiet-host and timing policy govern one full response. No quality or numerical gate is relaxed.

The raw coordinator and native receipt below contain the completed result. This is one cheap configuration control on the owned 48-GB Mac, not a paired quantization isolation experiment or another-Mac speed claim. The GSQ configuration retains a useful lead; the next frozen mechanism trial is candidate attention lookahead, with unchanged target arithmetic, exact state/output behavior and a five-percent useful-gain requirement.

The original model installation remains unchanged. No alternative enters production Auto or public distribution.
